| PROSPERO | Thou hadst, and more, [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]]. But how is it<br>That this lives in thy mind? What seest thou else<br>In the dark backward and abyss of [[motifs/time\|time]]?<br>If thou rememb‘rest aught ere thou cam’st here,<br>How thou cam’st here thou mayst. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | But that I do not. |
| PROSPERO | Twelve year since, [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]], twelve year since,<br>Thy father was the [[characters/duke-of-milan\|Duke of Milan]], and<br>A prince of power— |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Sir, are not you my father? |
| PROSPERO | Thy mother was a piece of virtue, and<br>She said thou wast my daughter; and thy father<br>Was [[characters/duke-of-milan\|Duke of Milan]], and his only heir<br>And princess no worse issued. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | O the heavens!<br>What foul play had we that we came from thence?<br>Or blessed was’t we did? |
| PROSPERO | Both, both, my girl.<br>By foul play, as thou sayst, were we heaved thence,<br>But blessedly holp hither. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | O, my heart bleeds<br>To think o’th’ teen that I have turned you to,<br>Which is from my remembrance. Please you, farther. |
| PROSPERO | My brother and thy uncle called [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]]—<br>I pray thee mark me, that a brother should<br>Be so perfidious—he whom next thyself<br>Of all the world I loved, and to him put<br>The manage of my state—as at that [[motifs/time\|time]]<br>Through all the signories it was the first,<br>And Prospero the prime duke—being so reputed<br>In dignity, and for the liberal arts<br>Without a parallel—those being all my study,<br>The government I cast upon my brother,<br>And to my state grew stranger, being transported<br>And rapt in secret studies. Thy false uncle—<br>Dost thou attend me? |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Sir, most heedfully. |
| PROSPERO | Being once perfected how to grant suits,<br>How to deny them, who t‘advance and who<br>To trash for over-topping, new created<br>The creatures that were mine, I say—or changed ’em<br>Or else new formed ’em; having both the key<br>Of officer and office, set all hearts i’th’ state<br>To what tune pleased his ear, that now he was<br>The ivy which had hid my princely trunk<br>And sucked my verdure out on’t. Thou attend’st not! |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | O good sir, I do. |
| PROSPERO | I pray thee mark me.<br>I, thus neglecting worldly ends, all dedicated<br>To closeness and the bettering of my mind<br>With that which but by being so retired<br>O‘er-priced all popular rate, in my false brother<br>Awaked an evil [[concepts/nature\|nature]]; and my trust,<br>Like a good parent, did beget of him<br>A falsehood, in its contrary as great<br>As my trust was, which had indeed no limit,<br>A confidence sans bound. He being thus lorded<br>Not only with what my revenue yielded<br>But what my power might else exact, like one<br>Who having into [[concepts/truth\|truth]], by telling oft,<br>Made such a sinner of his [[motifs/memory\|memory]]<br>To credit his own lie, he did believe<br>He was indeed the Duke. Out o’th’ substitution,<br>And executing th’outward face of royalty<br>With all prerogative, hence his [[concepts/ambition\|ambition]] growing—<br>Dost thou hear? |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Your tale, sir, would cure deafness. |
| PROSPERO | To have no screen between this part he played<br>And him he played it for, he needs will be<br>Absolute Milan. Me, poor man—my library<br>Was dukedom large enough—of temporal royalties<br>He thinks me now incapable; confederates,<br>So dry he was for sway, wi’th’ [[archetypes/king\|King]] of Naples<br>To give him annual tribute, do him homage,<br>Subject his coronet to his [[motifs/crown\|crown]], and bend<br>The dukedom, yet unbowed—alas,poor Milan—<br>To most ignoble stooping. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | O the heavens! |
| PROSPERO | Mark his condition and th’event, then tell me<br>If this might be a brother. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | I should sin<br>To think but nobly of my grandmother.<br>Good wombs have borne bad sons. |
| PROSPERO | Now the condition.<br>This [[archetypes/king\|King]] of Naples, being an enemy<br>To me inveterate, hearkens my brother’s suit;<br>Which was that he, in lieu o‘th’ premises<br>Of homage and I know not how much tribute,<br>Should presently extirpate me and mine<br>Out of the dukedom, and confer fair Milan,<br>With all the honours, on my brother. Whereon,<br>A treacherous army levied, one midnight<br>Fated to th’ purpose did [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]] open<br>The gates of Milan; and, i’th’ dead of darkness,<br>The ministers for th’ purpose hurried thence<br>Me and thy crying [[concepts/self\|self]]. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Alack, for pity!<br>I, not rememb’ring how I cried out then,<br>Will cry it o’er again; it is a hint<br>That wrings mine eyes to’t. |
| *(didascalia)* | PROSPERO sitting |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Hear a little further,<br>And then I’ll bring thee to the present business<br>Which now’s upon’s, without the which this story<br>Were most impertinent. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Wherefore did they not<br>That hour destroy us? |
| PROSPERO | [[motifs/well\|Well]] demanded, wench;<br>My tale provokes that question. Dear, they durst not,<br>So dear the [[motifs/love\|love]] my people bore me; nor set<br>A mark so bloody on the business, but<br>With colours fairer painted their foul ends.<br>In few, they hurried us aboard a barque,<br>Bore us some leagues to sea, where they prepared<br>A rotten carcass of a butt, not rigged,<br>Nor tackle, sail, nor mast—the very rats<br>Instinctively have quit it. There they hoist us,<br>To cry to th’ sea that roared to us, to sigh<br>To th’winds, whose pity, sighing back again,<br>Did us but loving wrong. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Alack, what trouble<br>Was I then to you! |
| PROSPERO | O,a cherubin<br>Thou wast that did preserve me. Thou didst smile,<br>Infused with a fortitude from heaven,<br>When I have decked the sea with drops full salt,<br>Under my burden groaned; which raised in me<br>An undergoing stomach, to bear up<br>Against what should ensue. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | How came we ashore? |
| PROSPERO | By [[concepts/providence\|providence]] divine.<br>Some food we had, and some fresh [[motifs/water\|water]], that<br>A noble Neapolitan, [[characters/gonzalo\|Gonzalo]],<br>Out of his [[concepts/charity\|charity]]—who being then appointed<br>Master of this design—did give us; with<br>Rich garments, linens, stuffs, and necessaries<br>Which since have steaded much. So, of his gentleness,<br>Knowing I loved my books, he furnished me<br>From mine own library with volumes that<br>I prize above my dukedom. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Would I might<br>But ever see that man! |
| PROSPERO | Now I arise. |
| *(didascalia)* | He stands and puts on his cloak |
| PROSPERO | Sit still, and hear the last of our sea-sorrow.<br>Here in this island we arrived, and here<br>Have I thy schoolmaster made thee more profit<br>Than other princes can, that have more [[motifs/time\|time]]<br>For vainer hours and tutors not so careful. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Heavens thank you for’t. And now I pray you, sir—<br>For still ’tis beating in my mind—your reason<br>For raising this sea-[[motifs/storm\|storm]]. |
| PROSPERO | Know thus far forth.<br>By accident most strange, bountiful Fortune,<br>Now my dear lady, hath mine enemies<br>Brought to this shore; and by my prescience<br>I find my zenith doth depend upon<br>A most auspicious star, whose influence<br>If now I [[settings/court\|court]] not, but omit, my fortunes<br>Will ever after droop. Here cease more questions.<br>Thou art inclined to [[motifs/sleep\|sleep]]; ’tis a good dullness,<br>And give it way. I know thou canst not choose.<br>[[characters/miranda\|Miranda]] sleeps<br>Come away, servant, come! I am ready now.<br>Approach, my [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]], come! |
| *(didascalia)* | Enter [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]] |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | All hail, great master, [[motifs/grave\|grave]] sir, hail. I come<br>To answer thy best pleasure. Be’t to fly,<br><br>To swim, to dive into the [[motifs/fire\|fire]], to ride<br>On the curled clouds, to thy strong bidding task<br>[[characters/ariel\|Ariel]] and all his quality. |
| PROSPERO | Hast thou, spirit,<br>Performed to point the tempest that I bade thee? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | To every article.<br>I boarded the [[archetypes/king\|King]]’s [[motifs/ship\|ship]]. Now on the beak,<br>Now in the waste, the deck, in every cabin,<br>I flamed amazement. Sometime I’d divide,<br>And burn in many places; on the top-mast,<br>The yards, and bowsprit, would I flame distinctly;<br>Then meet and join. Jove’s [[motifs/lightning\|lightning]], the precursors<br>O’th’ dreadful thunderclaps, more momentary<br>And sight-outrunning were not. The [[motifs/fire\|fire]] and cracks<br>Of sulphurous roaring the most mighty Neptune<br>Seem to besiege, and make his bold waves tremble,<br>Yea, his dread trident shake. |
| PROSPERO | My brave spirit!<br>Who was so firm, so constant, that this coil<br>Would not infect his reason? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Not a [[motifs/soul\|soul]]<br>But felt a fever of the mad, and played<br>Some tricks of desperation. All but mariners<br>Plunged in the foaming brine and quit the vessel,<br>Then all afire with me. The [[archetypes/king\|King]]’s son [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]],<br>With hair upstaring—then like reeds, not hair—<br>Was the first man that leaped; cried ‘Hell is empty,<br>And all the devils are here’. |
| PROSPERO | Why, that’s my spirit!<br>But was not this nigh shore? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Close by, my master. |
| PROSPERO | But are they, [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]], safe? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Not a hair perished.<br>On their sustaining garments not a blemish,<br>But fresher than before. And, as thou bad’st me,<br>In troops I have dispersed them ’bout the isle.<br>The [[archetypes/king\|King]]’s son have I landed by himself,<br>Whom I left cooling of the air with sighs<br>In an odd angle of the isle, and sitting,<br>His arms in this sad knot. |
| PROSPERO | Of the [[archetypes/king\|King]]’s [[motifs/ship\|ship]],<br>The mariners, say how thou hast disposed,<br>And all the rest o’th’ fleet. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Safely in harbour<br>Is the [[archetypes/king\|King]]’s [[motifs/ship\|ship]], in the deep nook where cnce<br>Thou called‘st me up at midnight to fetch dew<br>From the still-vexed Bermudas, there she’s hid;<br>The mariners all under hatches stowed,<br>Who, with a charm joined to their suffered labour,<br>I have left asleep. And for the rest o’th’ fleet,<br>Which I dispersed, they all have met again,<br>And are upon the Mediterranean float<br>Bound sadly [[motifs/home\|home]] for Naples,<br>Supposing that they saw the [[archetypes/king\|King]]’s [[motifs/ship\|ship]] wrecked,<br>And his great person perish. |
| PROSPERO | [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]], thy charge<br>Exactly is performed; but there’s more work.<br>What is the [[motifs/time\|time]] o’th’ day? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Past the mid season. |
| PROSPERO | At least two glasses. The [[motifs/time\|time]] ’twixt six and now<br>Must by us both be spent most preciously. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Is there more toil? Since thou dost give me pains,<br>Let me remember thee what thou hast promised<br>Which is not yet performed me. |
| PROSPERO | How now? Moody?<br>What is’t thou canst demand? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | My liberty. |
| PROSPERO | Before the [[motifs/time\|time]] be out? No more! |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | I prithee,<br>Remember I have done thee worthy service,<br>Told thee no lies, made thee no mistakings, served<br>Without or grudge or grumblings. Thou did promise<br>To bate me a full year. |
| PROSPERO | Dost thou forget<br>From what a torment I did free thee? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | No. |
| PROSPERO | Thou dost, and think‘st it much to tread the ooze<br>Of the salt deep,<br>To run upon the sharp [[motifs/wind\|wind]] of the north,<br>To do me business in the veins o’th’ earth<br>When it is baked with [[motifs/frost\|frost]]. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | I do not, sir. |
| PROSPERO | Thou liest, malignant thing. Hast thou forgot<br>The foul [[characters/witch\|witch]] Sycorax, who with age and envy<br>Was grown into a hoop? Hast thou forgot her? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | No, sir. |
| PROSPERO | Thou hast. Where was she born? Speak, tell me! |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Sir, in Algiers. |
| PROSPERO | O, was she so! I must<br>Once in a month recount what thou hast been,<br>Which thou forget‘st. This damned [[characters/witch\|witch]] Sycorax,<br>For mischiefs manifold and sorceries terrible<br>To enter human hearing, from Algiers<br>Thou know’st was banished. For one thing she did<br>They would not take her life. Is not this true? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Ay, sir. |
| PROSPERO | This blue-eyed hag was hither brought with [[archetypes/child\|child]],<br>And here was left by th’ sailors. Thou, my slave,<br>As thou report’st thyself, was then her servant;<br>And for thou wast a spirit too delicate<br>To act her earthy and abhorred commands,<br>Refusing her grand hests, she did confine thee<br>By help of her more potent ministers,<br>And in her most unmitigable rage,<br>Into a cloven pine; within which rift<br>Imprisoned thou didst painfully remain<br>A dozen years, within which space she died<br>And left thee there, where thou didst vent thy groans<br>As fast as mill-wheels strike. Then was this island—<br>Save for the son that she did litter here,<br>A freckled whelp, hag-born—not honoured with<br>A human shape. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Yes, [[characters/caliban\|Caliban]] her son. |
| PROSPERO | Dull thing, I say so: he, that [[characters/caliban\|Caliban]]<br>Whom now I keep in service. Thou best know’st<br>What torment I did find thee in. Thy groans<br>Did make wolves howl, and penetrate the breasts<br>Of ever-angry bears; it was a torment<br>To lay upon the damned, which Sycorax<br>Could not again undo. It was mine art,<br>When I arrived and heard thee, that made gape<br>The pine and let thee out. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | I thank thee, master. |
| PROSPERO | If thou more murmur’st, I will rend an oak,<br>And peg thee in his knotty entrails till<br>Thou hast howled away twelve winters. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Pardon, master.<br>I will be correspondent to command,<br>And do my spriting gently. |
| PROSPERO | Do so, and after two days<br>I will discharge thee. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | That’s my noble master!<br>What shall I do? Say what, what shall I do? |
| PROSPERO | Go make thyself like to a nymph o’th’ sea. Be subject<br>To no sight but thine and mine, invisible<br>To every eyeball else. Go take this shape,<br>And hither come in’t. Go; hence with diligence! |
| *(didascalia)* | Exit [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]] |
| PROSPERO | Awake, dear heart, awake! Thou hast slept [[motifs/well\|well]];<br>Awake. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | (awaking) The strangeness of your story put<br>Heaviness in me. |
| PROSPERO | Shake it off. Come on;<br>We’ll visit [[characters/caliban\|Caliban]] my slave, who never<br>Yields us kind answer. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | ’Tis a [[archetypes/villain\|villain]], sir,<br>I do not [[motifs/love\|love]] to look on. |
| PROSPERO | But as ’tis,<br>We cannot miss him. He does make our [[motifs/fire\|fire]],<br>Fetch in our wood, and serves in offices<br>That profit us.—What ho! Slave, [[characters/caliban\|Caliban]]!<br>Thou earth, thou, speak! |
| [[characters/caliban\|CALIBAN]] | (within)<br>There’s wood enough within. |
| PROSPERO | Come forth, I say! There’s other business for thee.<br>Come, thou tortoise! When? |
| *(didascalia)* | Enter [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]], like a [[motifs/water\|water]]-nymph |
| PROSPERO | Fine apparition! My quaint [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]],<br>Hark in thine ear.<br>He whispers |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | My lord, it shall be done. Exit |
| PROSPERO | Thou poisonous slave, got by the devil himself<br>Upon thy wicked dam, come forth! |
| *(didascalia)* | Enter [[characters/caliban\|Caliban]] |
| [[characters/caliban\|CALIBAN]] | As wicked dew as e’er my mother brushed<br>With raven’s feather from unwholesome fen<br>Drop on you both! A southwest blow on ye,<br>And blister you all o’er! |
| PROSPERO | For this be sure tonight thou shalt have cramps,<br>Side-stitches that shall pen thy breath up. Urchins<br>Shall forth at vast of [[motifs/night\|night]], that they may work<br>All exercise on thee. Thou shalt be pinched<br>As thick as honeycomb, each pinch more stinging<br>Than [[motifs/bees\|bees]] that made ’em. |
| [[characters/caliban\|CALIBAN]] | I must eat my dinner.<br>This island’s mine, by Sycorax my mother,<br>Which thou tak’st from me. When thou cam’st first,<br>Thou strok’st me and made much of me, wouldst give me<br>[[motifs/water\|Water]] with berries in’t, and teach me how<br>To name the bigger light, and how the less,<br>That burn by day and [[motifs/night\|night]]; and then I loved thee,<br>And showed thee all the qualities o’th’ isle,<br>The fresh springs, brine-pits, barren place and fertile—<br>Cursed be I that did so! All the charms<br>Of Sycorax, toads, beetles, bats, light on you;<br>For I am all the subjects that you have,<br>Which first was mine own [[archetypes/king\|king]], and here you sty me<br>In this hard rock, whiles you do keep from me<br>The rest o’th’ island. |
| PROSPERO | Thou most lying slave,<br>Whom stripes may move, not kindness! I have used<br>thee,<br>Filth as thou art, with human care, and lodged thee<br>In mine own cell, till thou didst seek to violate<br>The [[concepts/honour\|honour]] of my [[archetypes/child\|child]]. |
| [[characters/caliban\|CALIBAN]] | O ho,O ho! Would’t had been done!<br>Thou didst prevent me; I had peopled else<br>This isle with Calibans. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Abhorred slave,<br>Which any print of goodness wilt not take, Being capable of all ill! I pitied thee, Took pains to make thee speak, taught thee each hour One thing or other. When thou didst not, savage, Know thine own meaning, but wouldst gabble like A thing most brutish, I endowed thy purposes With words that made them known. But thy vile race, Though thou didst learn, had that in’t which good natures Could not abide to be with; therefore wast thou Deservedly confined into this rock, Who hadst deserved more than a [[motifs/prison\|prison]]. |
| [[characters/caliban\|CALIBAN]] | You taught me language, and my profit on’t<br>Is I know how to curse. The red plague rid you<br>For learning me your language! |
| PROSPERO | Hag-seed, hence!<br>Fetch us in fuel. And be quick, thou’rt best,<br>To answer other business.—Shrug’st thou, malice?<br>If thou neglect’st or dost unwillingly<br>What I command, I’ll rack thee with old cramps,<br>Fill all thy bones with aches, make thee roar,<br>That beasts shall tremble at thy din. |
| [[characters/caliban\|CALIBAN]] | No, pray thee.<br>(Aside) I must obey. His art is of such power<br>It would control my dam’s god Setebos,<br>And make a vassal of him. |
| PROSPERO | So, slave, hence! |
| *(didascalia)* | Exit [[characters/caliban\|Caliban]] |
| *(didascalia)* | Enter [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]] like a [[motifs/water\|water]]-nymph,playing and |
| PROSPERO | singing, invisible to [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]], who follows. |
| *(didascalia)* | Prospero and [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]] stand aside |
| *(didascalia)* | [[works/song-(poe)\|Song]] |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Come unto these yellow sands, And then take hands;<br>Curtsied when you have and kissed—<br>The wild waves whist—<br>Foot it featly here and there,<br>And, sweet sprites, bear<br>The burden. Hark, hark. |
| *(didascalia)* | SPIRITS (dispersedly within) |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Bow-wow! |
| *(didascalia)* | [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | The watch-dogs bark.<br>FSPIRITS] (within) Bow-wow! |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Hark, hark, I hear<br>The strain of strutting Chanticleer<br>Cry ‘cock-a-diddle-dow’. |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | Where should this music be? I’th’ air or th’earth?<br>It sounds no more; and sure it waits upon<br>Some god o‘th’ island. Sitting on a bank,<br>Weeping again the [[archetypes/king\|King]] my father’s wreck,<br>This music crept by me upon the waters,<br>Allaying both their fury and my passion<br>With its sweet air. Thence I have followed it—<br>Or it hath drawn me rather. But ’tis gone.<br>No, it begins again. |
| *(didascalia)* | [[works/song-(poe)\|Song]] |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Full fathom five thy father lies. Of his bones are coral made;<br>Those are pearls that were his eyes;<br>Nothing of him that doth fade<br>But doth suffer a sea-change<br>Into something rich and strange.<br>Sea-nymphs hourly ring his knell: |
| *(didascalia)* | SPIRITS(within) Ding dong. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Hark, now I hear them. |
| *(didascalia)* | SPIRITS(within) |
| *(didascalia)* | Ding-dong bell. etc. |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | The ditty does remember my drowned father.<br>This is no mortal business, nor no sound<br>That the earth owes. |
| *(didascalia)* | Music |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | I hear it now above me. |
| PROSPERO | (to [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]])<br>The fringed curtains of thine eye advance,<br>And say what thou seest yon. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | What is’t? A spirit?<br>Lord, how it looks about! Believe me, sir,<br>It carries a brave form. But ’tis a spirit. |
| PROSPERO | No, wench, it eats and sleeps, and hath such senses<br>As we have, such. This gallant which thou seest<br>Was in the wreck, and but he’s something stained<br>With grief, that’s [[concepts/beauty\|beauty]]’s canker, thou mightst call<br>him<br>A goodly person. He hath lost his fellows,<br>And strays about to find ’em. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | I might call him<br>A thing divine, for nothing natural<br>I ever saw so noble. |
| PROSPERO | (aside)<br>It goes on, I see,<br>As my [[motifs/soul\|soul]] prompts it. (To [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]]) Spirit, fine spirit, I’ll<br>free thee |
| *(didascalia)* | Within two days for this. |
| *(didascalia)* | [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] aside |
| PROSPERO | Most sure the goddess<br>On whom these airs attend. (To [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]]) Vouchsafe<br>my [[motifs/prayer\|prayer]] 425<br>May know if you remain upon this island,<br>And that you will some good instruction give<br>How I may bear me here. My prime request,<br>Which I do last pronounce, is—O you [[concepts/wonder\|wonder]]—<br>If you be maid or no? |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | No [[concepts/wonder\|wonder]], sir,<br>But certainly a maid. |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | My language! Heavens!<br>I am the best of them that speak this speech,<br>Were I but where ’tis spoken. |
| PROSPERO | How, the best?<br>What wert thou if the [[archetypes/king\|King]] of Naples heard thee? |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | A single thing, as I am now that wonders<br>To hear thee speak of Naples. He does hear me,<br>And that he does I weep. Myself am Naples,<br>Who with mine eyes, never since at ebb, beheld<br>The [[archetypes/king\|King]] my father wrecked. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Alack, for [[concepts/mercy\|mercy]]! |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | Yes, [[concepts/faith\|faith]], and all his lords, the [[characters/duke-of-milan\|Duke of Milan]]<br>And his brave son being twain. |
| PROSPERO | (aside)<br>The [[characters/duke-of-milan\|Duke of Milan]]<br>And his more braver daughter could control thee,<br>If now ’twere fit to do’t. At the first sight<br>They have changed eyes.—Deticate [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]],<br>I’ll set thee free for this. (To [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]]) A word, good<br>sir. 445<br>I [[concepts/fear\|fear]] you have done yourself some wrong. A word. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | (aside)<br>Why speaks my father so ungently? This<br>Is the third man that e’er I saw, the first<br>That e’er I sighed for. Pity move my father<br>To be inclined my way. |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | O, if a virgin,<br>And your affection not gone forth, I’ll make you<br>The [[characters/queen\|Queen]] of Naples. |
| PROSPERO | Soft, sir! One word more.<br>(Aside) They are both in either’s powers. But this swift<br>business<br>I must uneasy make, lest too light winning<br>Make the prize light. (To [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]]) One word more. I<br>charge thee<br>That thou attend me. Thou dost here usurp<br>The name thou ow’st not; and hast put thyself<br>Upon this island as a spy, to win it<br>From me the lord on’t. |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | No, as I am a man. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | There’s nothing ill can dwell in such a temple.<br>If the ill spirit have so fair a house,<br>Good things will strive to dwell with’t. |
| PROSPERO | (to [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]])<br>Follow me.<br>(To [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]]) Speak not you for him; he’s a traitor.<br>(To [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]]) Come!<br>I’ll manacle thy neck and feet together.<br>Sea-[[motifs/water\|water]] shalt thou drink; thy food shall be<br>The fresh-brook mussels, withered roots, and husks<br>Wherein the acorn cradled. Follow! |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | No.<br>I will resist such entertainment till<br>Mine enemy has more power.<br>He draws, and is charmed from moving |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | O dear father,<br>Make not too rash a trial of him, for<br>He’s gentle, and not fearful. |
| PROSPERO | What, I say,<br>My foot my tutor? Put thy sword up, traitor,<br>Who mak‘st a show but dar’st not strike, thy<br>[[concepts/conscience\|conscience]]<br>Is so possessed with [[concepts/guilt\|guilt]]. Come from thy ward,<br>For I can here disarm thee with this stick<br>And make thy weapon drop. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Beseech you, father! |
| PROSPERO | Hence! Hang not on my garments. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Sir, have pity.<br>I’ll be his surety. |
| PROSPERO | Silencel One word more<br>Shall make me chide thee, if not hate thee. What,<br>An advocate for an impostor? Hush!<br>Thou think’st there is no more such shapes as he,<br>Having seen but him and [[characters/caliban\|Caliban]]. Foolish wench!<br>To th’ most of men this is a [[characters/caliban\|Caliban]],<br>And they to him are angels. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | My affections<br>Are then most humble. I have no [[concepts/ambition\|ambition]]<br>To see a goodlier man. |
| PROSPERO | (to [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]]) Come on; obey.<br>Thy nerves are in their infancy again,<br>And have no vigour in them. |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | So they are.<br>My spirits, as in a dream, are all bound up.<br>My father’s loss, the weakness which I feel,<br>The wreck of all my friends, nor this man’s threats<br>To whom I am subdued, are but light to me,<br>Might I but through my [[motifs/prison\|prison]] once a day<br>Behold this maid. All corners else o’th’ earth<br>Let liberty make use of; space enough<br>Have I in such a [[motifs/prison\|prison]]. |
| PROSPERO | (aside)<br>It works. (To [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]]) Come on.—<br>Thou hast done [[motifs/well\|well]], fine [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]]. (To [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]]) Follow<br>me.<br>(To [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]]) Hark what thou else shalt do me. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | (to [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]])<br>Be of comfort.<br>My father’s of a better [[concepts/nature\|nature]], sir,<br>Than he appears by speech. This is unwonted<br>Which now came from him. |
| PROSPERO | (to [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]])<br>Thou shalt be as free<br>As mountain winds; but then exactly do<br>All points of my command. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | To th’ syllable. |
| PROSPERO | (to [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]])<br>Come, follow. (To [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]]) Speak not for him. |
| *(didascalia)* | Exeunt |


<span class="qlang-split" data-lang="it"></span>

| Chi parla | Battuta |
|---|---|
| *(didascalia)* | Entra Prospero nel suo mantello magico, con un bastone, e [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]] |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Se con la vostra arte, padre carissimo, avete<br>messo in questo fragore le acque selvagge, placatele.<br>Il cielo, pare, vorrebbe rovesciare pece fetida,<br>se non che il mare, salendo alla guancia del firmamento,<br>ne spegne il [[motifs/fire\|fuoco]]. Oh, ho sofferto<br>con quelli che ho visto soffrire! Un nobile vascello,<br>che aveva, senza [[concepts/doubt\|dubbio]], qualche nobile creatura a bordo,<br>fatto tutto in pezzi! Oh, quel grido percosse<br>il mio cuore stesso! Povere anime, sono perite.<br>Fossi stata un dio di potenza, avrei<br>sprofondato il mare dentro la terra, prima<br>che inghiottisse così la buona [[motifs/ship\|nave]] e<br>le anime che portava in grembo. |
| PROSPERO | Rasserénati.<br>Basta sgomento. Di' al tuo cuore pietoso<br>che nessun male è stato fatto. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Oh, giorno di sventura! |
| PROSPERO | Nessun male.<br>Nulla ho fatto se non per cura di te,<br>di te, mia cara, di te, figlia mia, che<br>ignori ciò che sei, nulla sapendo<br>di donde io venga, né che io sia altro e meglio<br>che Prospero, signore d'una assai povera cella<br>e tuo padre non più grande. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Saperne di più<br>non s'è mai immischiato coi miei pensieri. |
| PROSPERO | È [[motifs/time\|tempo]]<br>ch'io t'informi oltre. Porgi la mano,<br>e togli da me la mia veste magica. |
| *(didascalia)* | [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]] toglie a Prospero il mantello, ed egli lo depone |
| *(didascalia)* | a terra |
| PROSPERO | Così.<br>Giaci lì, arte mia.—Asciugati gli occhi; consòlati.<br>Il tremendo spettacolo del naufragio, che ha toccato<br>in te la virtù stessa della compassione,<br>io l'ho con tale previdenza nella mia arte<br>ordinato al sicuro che non v'è [[motifs/soul\|anima]]—<br>no, non tanta perdita quanto un capello<br>è occorsa ad alcuna creatura di quel vascello,<br>che udisti gridare, che vedesti affondare. Siedi<br>qui,<br>ché ora devi saperne di più.<br>[[characters/miranda\|Miranda]] si siede |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Spesso avete<br>cominciato a dirmi ciò che sono, ma vi siete fermato<br>lasciandomi a una vana [[historical-references/inquisition\|inchiesta]],<br>concludendo: «Férmati; non ancora». |
| PROSPERO | L'ora è venuta.<br>Il minuto stesso ti comanda d'aprire l'orecchio,<br>d'obbedire e d'essere attenta. Puoi ricordare<br>un [[motifs/time\|tempo]] prima che venissimo a questa cella?<br>Non credo che tu possa, ché allora non avevi<br>compiuto i tre anni. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Certamente, signore, posso. |
| PROSPERO | Da che? Da qualche altra casa o persona?<br>Di qualunque cosa dimmi l'immagine<br>che sia rimasta nel tuo ricordo. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | È lontano,<br>e più simile a un sogno che a una certezza<br>garantita dalla mia memoria. Non avevo<br>quattro o cinque donne, un tempo, che mi accudivano? |
| PROSPERO | Le avevi, e di più, [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]]. Ma com'è<br>che questo vive nella tua mente? Che altro vedi<br>nel buio retrocedere e nell'abisso del [[motifs/time\|tempo]]?<br>Se ricordi qualcosa prima che qui giungessi,<br>puoi ricordare come qui giungesti. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Ma questo no. |
| PROSPERO | Dodici anni fa, [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]], dodici anni fa,<br>tuo padre era il [[characters/duke-of-milan\|Duca di Milano]], e<br>un principe di potenza— |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Signore, non siete voi mio padre? |
| PROSPERO | Tua madre era un modello di virtù, e<br>disse che tu eri mia figlia; e tuo padre<br>era [[characters/duke-of-milan\|Duca di Milano]], e la sua unica erede<br>principessa di non minore nascita. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Oh cieli!<br>Quale trama scellerata ci portò via di là?<br>O fu benedizione che ne venissimo? |
| PROSPERO | Entrambe, entrambe, bambina mia.<br>Per trama scellerata, come dici, di là fummo cacciati,<br>ma benedettamente sospinti qui. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Oh, mi sanguina il cuore<br>a pensare all'affanno cui vi ho costretto,<br>che è fuori dal mio ricordo. Vi prego, proseguite. |
| PROSPERO | Mio fratello e tuo zio, chiamato [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]]—<br>ti prego, ascoltami: che un fratello possa<br>essere così perfido!—lui che dopo di te<br>amavo più d'ogni altro al mondo, e a lui affidai<br>la gestione del mio stato—che a quel [[motifs/time\|tempo]]<br>fra tutte le signorie era il primo,<br>e Prospero il duca sovrano—così reputato<br>in dignità, e per le arti liberali<br>senza pari—ed essendo quelle tutto il mio studio,<br>il governo rovesciai su mio fratello,<br>e al mio stato divenni estraneo, rapito<br>e assorto in studi segreti. Il tuo falso zio—<br>mi stai ascoltando? |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Signore, con la massima attenzione. |
| PROSPERO | Perfezionatosi una volta nel concedere le suppliche,<br>nel negarle, chi promuovere e chi<br>potare perché troppo s'innalza, ricreò<br>le creature che erano mie, dico—o le mutò<br>o le riformò daccapo; avendo la chiave<br>e dell'ufficiale e dell'ufficio, accordò tutti i cuori dello stato<br>sul tono che piaceva al suo orecchio, sicché ormai egli era<br>l'edera che aveva nascosto il mio tronco principesco<br>e ne aveva succhiato via il verde. Non mi ascolti! |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Oh, buon signore, vi ascolto. |
| PROSPERO | Ti prego, ascoltami.<br>Io, trascurando così i fini mondani, tutto dedito<br>al ritiro e al perfezionamento della mia mente<br>con ciò che, solo per essere tanto appartato,<br>superava ogni stima comune, nel mio falso fratello<br>destai una malvagia [[concepts/nature\|natura]]; e la mia fiducia,<br>come un buon padre, generò in lui<br>una falsità grande, nel suo contrario, quanto<br>grande era la mia fiducia, che davvero non aveva limite,<br>una confidenza senza confini. Fatto egli così signore<br>non solo di quanto rendeva la mia entrata,<br>ma di quanto altro il mio potere potesse esigere, come colui<br>che, della [[concepts/truth\|verità]], a forza di ripeterla,<br>fa tal peccatrice della propria [[motifs/memory\|memoria]]<br>da prestar fede alla sua stessa menzogna, credette<br>d'essere davvero il Duca. Dalla sostituzione,<br>e dall'esercitare la faccia esteriore della regalità<br>con ogni prerogativa, crebbe quindi la sua [[concepts/ambition\|ambizione]]—<br>mi senti? |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Il vostro racconto, signore, guarirebbe la sordità. |
| PROSPERO | Per non aver schermo tra la parte che recitava<br>e colui per cui la recitava, volle a ogni costo essere<br>Milano assoluto. Me, pover'uomo—la mia biblioteca<br>era ducato abbastanza grande—delle regalità temporali<br>mi giudica ormai incapace; s'accorda,<br>tanta sete aveva di dominio, col [[archetypes/king\|Re]] di Napoli<br>per dargli tributo annuo, rendergli omaggio,<br>sottomettere la sua coroncina alla [[motifs/crown\|corona]] di lui, e piegare<br>il ducato, finora mai piegato—ahimè, povera Milano—<br>al più ignobile inchino. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Oh cieli! |
| PROSPERO | Nota la sua condizione e l'esito, poi dimmi<br>se questi possa essere un fratello. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Peccherei<br>a non pensare nobilmente di mia nonna.<br>Buoni grembi han partorito cattivi figli. |
| PROSPERO | Ora la condizione.<br>Questo [[archetypes/king\|Re]] di Napoli, essendo nemico<br>mio inveterato, dà ascolto alla supplica di mio fratello;<br>la quale era che egli, in cambio delle premesse<br>d'omaggio e non so quanto tributo,<br>dovesse all'istante estirpare me e i miei<br>fuori dal ducato, e conferire la bella Milano,<br>con tutti gli onori, a mio fratello. Onde,<br>levato un esercito traditore, una mezzanotte<br>fatata allo scopo [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]] aprì<br>le porte di Milano; e, nel fitto del buio,<br>i ministri deputati allo scopo di là trascinarono<br>me e il tuo piangente [[concepts/self\|io]]. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Ahimè, che pietà!<br>Io, non ricordando come allora gridai,<br>tornerò a gridarlo; è uno spunto<br>che mi strizza gli occhi a farlo. |
| *(didascalia)* | PROSPERO si siede |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Ascolta ancora un poco,<br>e poi ti condurrò all'affare presente<br>che ora ci incombe, senza il quale questa storia<br>sarebbe del tutto impertinente. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Perché non ci distrussero<br>in quell'ora? |
| PROSPERO | [[motifs/well\|Bene]] domandato, ragazza;<br>il mio racconto provoca quella domanda. Cara, non osarono,<br>tanto caro era l'[[motifs/love\|amore]] che il mio popolo mi portava; né posero<br>un marchio così sanguinoso sull'impresa, ma<br>con colori più belli dipinsero i loro turpi fini.<br>In breve, ci trascinarono a bordo d'una barca,<br>ci portarono qualche lega al largo, dove avevano preparato<br>la carcassa marcita d'un barile, senza sartiame,<br>né gomene, né vela, né albero—i topi stessi<br>per istinto l'avevano abbandonata. Là ci issarono,<br>a gridare al mare che ci ruggiva, a sospirare<br>ai venti, la cui pietà, risospirando,<br>non ci fece che un amoroso torto. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Ahimè, che pena<br>fui allora per voi! |
| PROSPERO | Oh, un cherubino<br>tu fosti, che mi preservò. Tu sorridevi,<br>infusa d'una fortezza venuta dal cielo,<br>quando io adornavo il mare di gocce salatissime,<br>gemendo sotto il mio peso; il che destò in me<br>un animo capace di sopportare, per reggere<br>contro ciò che doveva venire. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Come giungemmo a riva? |
| PROSPERO | Per divina [[concepts/providence\|provvidenza]].<br>Un po' di cibo avemmo, e un po' d'[[motifs/water\|acqua]] dolce, che<br>un nobile napoletano, [[characters/gonzalo\|Gonzalo]],<br>per la sua [[concepts/charity\|carità]]—essendo allora designato<br>esecutore di questo disegno—ci diede; con<br>ricche vesti, biancherie, stoffe e necessari<br>che poi ci han giovato molto. Così, per la sua gentilezza,<br>sapendo che amavo i miei libri, mi fornì<br>dalla mia stessa biblioteca volumi che<br>apprezzo più del mio ducato. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Potessi io<br>vedere una volta quell'uomo! |
| PROSPERO | Ora mi alzo. |
| *(didascalia)* | Si alza e indossa il mantello |
| PROSPERO | Resta seduta, e odi l'ultimo del nostro dolore marino.<br>Qui in quest'isola approdammo, e qui<br>io, tuo maestro, t'ho fatta profittare più<br>di quanto possano altri principi, che hanno più [[motifs/time\|tempo]]<br>per ore più vane e precettori non così solleciti. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Il cielo ve ne renda grazie. E ora vi prego, signore—<br>ché ancora mi batte nella mente—la vostra ragione<br>per aver sollevato questa [[motifs/storm\|tempesta]] marina. |
| PROSPERO | Sappi fin qui.<br>Per accidente stranissimo, la generosa Fortuna,<br>ora mia cara signora, i miei nemici<br>ha portato a questa riva; e per la mia prescienza<br>scopro che il mio zenit dipende<br>da una stella assai propizia, il cui influsso<br>se ora non [[settings/court\|corteggio]], ma trascuro, le mie sorti<br>per sempre appresso languiranno. Qui cessino altre domande.<br>Sei inclinata al [[motifs/sleep\|sonno]]; è un buon torpore,<br>e lasciagli via libera. So che non puoi scegliere.<br>[[characters/miranda\|Miranda]] dorme<br>Vieni, servo, vieni! Sono pronto adesso.<br>Accòstati, mio [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]], vieni! |
| *(didascalia)* | Entra [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]] |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Salve, gran padrone, [[motifs/grave\|grave]] signore, salve. Vengo<br>a rispondere al tuo miglior piacere. Sia volare,<br><br>nuotare, tuffarsi nel [[motifs/fire\|fuoco]], cavalcare<br>sulle nubi arricciate: al tuo forte comando metti all'opera<br>[[characters/ariel\|Ariel]] e tutta la sua schiera. |
| PROSPERO | Hai tu, spirito,<br>eseguito a puntino la tempesta che t'ordinai? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | In ogni articolo.<br>Salii sulla [[motifs/ship\|nave]] del [[archetypes/king\|Re]]. Ora sul rostro,<br>ora a mezza nave, sul ponte, in ogni cabina,<br>fiammeggiai sgomento. Talora mi dividevo,<br>e ardevo in molti luoghi; sull'albero di gabbia,<br>sui pennoni e sul bompresso fiammeggiavo distinto;<br>poi mi riunivo. Le [[motifs/lightning\|folgori]] di Giove, precorritrici<br>dei tremendi tuoni, non furono mai più istantanee<br>né più rapide della vista. Il [[motifs/fire\|fuoco]] e gli schianti<br>del ruggito sulfureo parvero assediare<br>il potentissimo Nettuno, e far tremare le sue onde ardite,<br>sì, e vacillare il suo temuto tridente. |
| PROSPERO | Mio bravo spirito!<br>Chi fu così fermo, così costante, che questo tumulto<br>non infettasse la sua ragione? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Non un'[[motifs/soul\|anima]]<br>che non sentisse una febbre da folli, e non facesse<br>qualche atto di disperazione. Tutti fuorché i marinai<br>si tuffarono nella salsedine spumosa e lasciarono il vascello,<br>tutto in fiamme con me. Il figlio del [[archetypes/king\|Re]], [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]],<br>coi capelli ritti—canne allora, non capelli—<br>fu il primo a saltare; gridò: «L'inferno è vuoto,<br>e tutti i diavoli sono qui». |
| PROSPERO | Ecco, questo è il mio spirito!<br>Ma non era vicino a riva? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Vicinissimo, padrone mio. |
| PROSPERO | Ma sono, [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]], salvi? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Non un capello è perito.<br>Sulle vesti che li sostennero non una macchia,<br>anzi più fresche di prima. E, come m'ordinasti,<br>a gruppi li ho dispersi per l'isola.<br>Il figlio del [[archetypes/king\|Re]] l'ho sbarcato tutto solo,<br>e l'ho lasciato a rinfrescar l'aria di sospiri<br>in uno strano angolo dell'isola, seduto,<br>le braccia in questo triste nodo. |
| PROSPERO | Della [[motifs/ship\|nave]] del [[archetypes/king\|Re]],<br>dei marinai, di' come hai disposto,<br>e di tutto il resto della flotta. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Al sicuro nel porto<br>è la [[motifs/ship\|nave]] del [[archetypes/king\|Re]], nella profonda insenatura dove una volta<br>mi chiamasti a mezzanotte a coglier rugiada<br>dalle sempre tempestose Bermude: là è nascosta;<br>i marinai tutti stivati sotto i boccaporti,<br>che, con un incanto unito alla fatica sofferta,<br>ho lasciato addormentati. E quanto al resto della flotta,<br>che dispersi, si sono tutti ritrovati,<br>e sull'onda mediterranea navigano<br>tristemente diretti a [[motifs/home\|casa]], verso Napoli,<br>supponendo d'aver visto la [[motifs/ship\|nave]] del [[archetypes/king\|Re]] naufragata<br>e perire la sua gran persona. |
| PROSPERO | [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]], il tuo incarico<br>è eseguito esattamente; ma c'è altro lavoro.<br>Che [[motifs/time\|ora]] è del giorno? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Passato il mezzo del giorno. |
| PROSPERO | Almeno due ampolle. Il [[motifs/time\|tempo]] fra le sei e adesso<br>dev'essere da noi due speso preziosamente. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | C'è altra fatica? Poiché mi dai travagli,<br>lascia che ti rammenti ciò che hai promesso<br>e che non m'è stato ancora adempiuto. |
| PROSPERO | E che? Di malumore?<br>Che cosa puoi pretendere? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | La mia libertà. |
| PROSPERO | Prima che il [[motifs/time\|tempo]] sia scaduto? Basta! |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Ti prego,<br>ricorda che t'ho reso degno servizio,<br>non t'ho detto bugie, non t'ho fatto errori, ho servito<br>senza rancore né brontolii. Tu promettesti<br>di scontarmi un anno intero. |
| PROSPERO | Dimentichi<br>da quale tormento ti liberai? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | No. |
| PROSPERO | Sì, e ti pare gran cosa calcare la melma<br>del salso abisso,<br>correre sull'aspro [[motifs/wind\|vento]] del nord,<br>sbrigarmi faccende nelle vene della terra<br>quando è indurita dal [[motifs/frost\|gelo]]. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Non è così, signore. |
| PROSPERO | Menti, cosa maligna. Hai dimenticato<br>la turpe [[characters/witch\|strega]] Sicorace, che per età e invidia<br>s'era piegata in cerchio? L'hai dimenticata? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | No, signore. |
| PROSPERO | Sì. Dov'è nata? Parla, dimmelo! |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Signore, in Algeri. |
| PROSPERO | Oh, era così! Devo<br>una volta al mese raccontarti ciò che sei stato,<br>e che dimentichi. Questa dannata [[characters/witch\|strega]] Sicorace,<br>per malefici molteplici e stregonerie terribili<br>a entrare in umano udito, da Algeri<br>sai che fu bandita. Per una cosa che fece<br>non le tolsero la vita. Non è forse vero? |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Sì, signore. |
| PROSPERO | Questa megera dagli occhi lividi fu portata qui gravida d'un [[archetypes/child\|figlio]],<br>e qui fu lasciata dai marinai. Tu, mio schiavo,<br>come tu stesso riferisci, eri allora suo servo;<br>e poiché eri uno spirito troppo delicato<br>per eseguire i suoi terrosi e aborriti comandi,<br>rifiutando i suoi grandi ordini, ella ti confinò<br>con l'aiuto dei suoi ministri più potenti,<br>e nella sua rabbia più inesorabile,<br>dentro un pino spaccato; nella qual fenditura<br>imprigionato dolorosamente rimanesti<br>una dozzina d'anni, nel qual tempo ella morì<br>e ti lasciò là, dove sfogavi i tuoi gemiti<br>fitti come i colpi delle ruote di mulino. Allora quest'isola—<br>salvo il figlio ch'ella figliò qui,<br>un cucciolo lentigginoso, nato da strega—non era onorata<br>di forma umana. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Sì, [[characters/caliban\|Calibano]] suo figlio. |
| PROSPERO | Cosa ottusa, così dico: lui, quel [[characters/caliban\|Calibano]]<br>che ora tengo al mio servizio. Tu sai meglio d'ogni altro<br>in quale tormento ti trovai. I tuoi gemiti<br>facevano ululare i lupi, e penetravano i petti<br>degli orsi sempre iracondi; era un tormento<br>da infliggere ai dannati, che Sicorace<br>non poté più disfare. Fu la mia arte,<br>quando giunsi e t'udii, a far spalancare<br>il pino e a lasciarti uscire. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Ti ringrazio, padrone. |
| PROSPERO | Se mormori ancora, squarcerò una quercia,<br>e t'inchioderò nelle sue nodose viscere finché<br>non avrai ululato via dodici inverni. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Perdono, padrone.<br>Sarò conforme al comando,<br>e farò il mio ufficio di spirito docilmente. |
| PROSPERO | Fa' così, e dopo due giorni<br>ti congederò. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Ecco il mio nobile padrone!<br>Che devo fare? Di' che cosa, che devo fare? |
| PROSPERO | Va', fatti simile a una ninfa del mare. Non essere visibile<br>ad altra vista che alla tua e alla mia, invisibile<br>a ogni altra pupilla. Va', prendi questa forma,<br>e in essa torna qui. Va'; via, con diligenza! |
| *(didascalia)* | Esce [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]] |
| PROSPERO | Svegliati, cuore caro, svegliati! Hai dormito [[motifs/well\|bene]];<br>svegliati. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | (destandosi) La stranezza del vostro racconto ha messo<br>pesantezza in me. |
| PROSPERO | Scuotila via. Andiamo;<br>visiteremo [[characters/caliban\|Calibano]], mio schiavo, che mai<br>ci rende gentile risposta. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | È un [[archetypes/villain\|furfante]], signore,<br>che non [[motifs/love\|amo]] guardare. |
| PROSPERO | Ma per come stanno le cose,<br>non possiamo farne a meno. Egli accende il nostro [[motifs/fire\|fuoco]],<br>porta dentro la legna, e serve in uffici<br>che ci giovano.—Ehilà! Schiavo, [[characters/caliban\|Calibano]]!<br>Tu, terra, tu, parla! |
| [[characters/caliban\|CALIBAN]] | (da dentro)<br>C'è legna abbastanza qui dentro. |
| PROSPERO | Vieni fuori, dico! C'è altra faccenda per te.<br>Vieni, tartaruga! Quando? |
| *(didascalia)* | Entra [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]], come ninfa dell'[[motifs/water\|acqua]] |
| PROSPERO | Bella apparizione! Mio grazioso [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]],<br>ascolta nel tuo orecchio.<br>Gli sussurra |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Mio signore, sarà fatto. Esce |
| PROSPERO | Tu, schiavo velenoso, generato dal diavolo in persona<br>sulla tua scellerata madre, vieni fuori! |
| *(didascalia)* | Entra [[characters/caliban\|Calibano]] |
| [[characters/caliban\|CALIBAN]] | Rugiada malefica quanta mai mia madre ne raccolse<br>con penna di corvo da palude insalubre<br>cada su voi due! Un libeccio soffi su di voi,<br>e vi copra tutti di vesciche! |
| PROSPERO | Per questo, stanne certo, stanotte avrai crampi,<br>fitte al fianco che ti chiuderanno il respiro. Folletti<br>usciranno nel vuoto della [[motifs/night\|notte]], per esercitare<br>su di te ogni loro travaglio. Sarai pizzicato<br>fitto come un favo, ogni pizzico più pungente<br>delle [[motifs/bees\|api]] che l'han fatto. |
| [[characters/caliban\|CALIBAN]] | Devo mangiare il mio pranzo.<br>Quest'isola è mia, per Sicorace mia madre,<br>e tu me la togli. Quando venisti la prima volta,<br>mi accarezzavi e mi vezzeggiavi, mi davi<br>[[motifs/water\|acqua]] con dentro delle bacche, e m'insegnavi<br>a nominare la luce più grande, e come la minore,<br>che ardono di giorno e di [[motifs/night\|notte]]; e allora t'amai,<br>e ti mostrai tutte le qualità dell'isola,<br>le fonti fresche, le saline, il luogo sterile e il fertile—<br>maledetto io che lo feci! Tutti gli incanti<br>di Sicorace, rospi, scarafaggi, pipistrelli, cadano su di te;<br>ché io sono tutti i sudditi che hai,<br>io che prima ero [[archetypes/king\|re]] di me stesso, e qui mi rinchiudi<br>in questa dura roccia, mentre mi tieni lontano<br>dal resto dell'isola. |
| PROSPERO | Schiavo mendacissimo,<br>che le frustate muovono, non la bontà! Io t'ho usato,<br>te,<br>lordura qual sei, con umana cura, e t'ho alloggiato<br>nella mia stessa cella, finché non cercasti di violare<br>l'[[concepts/honour\|onore]] della mia [[archetypes/child\|figlia]]. |
| [[characters/caliban\|CALIBAN]] | Oh oh, oh oh! Fosse stato fatto!<br>Tu me l'impedisti; altrimenti avrei popolato<br>quest'isola di Calibani. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Schiavo aborrito,<br>che nessuna impronta di bontà vuoi ricevere, capace d'ogni male! Ebbi pietà di te, mi diedi pena d'insegnarti a parlare, t'insegnai ogni ora una cosa o l'altra. Quando tu, selvaggio, non conoscevi il tuo stesso senso, ma farfugliavi come cosa brutissima, dotai i tuoi intenti di parole che li rendessero noti. Ma la tua vile razza, benché tu imparassi, aveva in sé ciò con cui le buone nature non potevano sopportare di stare; perciò fosti meritatamente confinato in questa roccia, tu che avevi meritato più d'una [[motifs/prison\|prigione]]. |
| [[characters/caliban\|CALIBAN]] | M'insegnaste la lingua, e il mio guadagno<br>è che so maledire. La peste rossa vi porti via<br>per avermi insegnato la vostra lingua! |
| PROSPERO | Seme di strega, via!<br>Portaci legna. E sii svelto, ti conviene,<br>per attendere ad altre faccende.—Alzi le spalle, malizia?<br>Se trascuri o fai di malavoglia<br>ciò che comando, ti torturerò con vecchi crampi,<br>ti riempirò le ossa di dolori, ti farò ruggire<br>sì che le bestie tremeranno al tuo fracasso. |
| [[characters/caliban\|CALIBAN]] | No, ti prego.<br>(A parte) Devo obbedire. La sua arte è di tal potere<br>che dominerebbe Setebo, il dio di mia madre,<br>e ne farebbe un vassallo. |
| PROSPERO | Così, schiavo, via! |
| *(didascalia)* | Esce [[characters/caliban\|Calibano]] |
| *(didascalia)* | Entra [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]] come ninfa dell'[[motifs/water\|acqua]], suonando e |
| PROSPERO | cantando, invisibile a [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]], che lo segue. |
| *(didascalia)* | Prospero e [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]] si tengono in disparte |
| *(didascalia)* | Canzone |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Venite su queste sabbie gialle, e poi datevi la mano;<br>fatta la riverenza e dato il bacio—<br>tacciano le onde selvagge—<br>danzate lievi qua e là,<br>e voi, dolci spiriti, reggete<br>il ritornello. Udite, udite. |
| *(didascalia)* | SPIRITI (sparsi, da dentro) |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Bau-bau! |
| *(didascalia)* | [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | I cani da guardia abbaiano.<br>[SPIRITI] (da dentro) Bau-bau! |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Udite, udite, sento<br>il canto di Chanticleer che si pavoneggia<br>gridare «chicchirichì». |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | Dove sarà questa musica? Nell'aria o nella terra?<br>Non suona più; e certo essa fa corteo<br>a qualche dio dell'isola. Sedendo su una riva,<br>piangendo di nuovo il naufragio del [[archetypes/king\|Re]] mio padre,<br>questa musica mi strisciò accanto sulle acque,<br>placando e la loro furia e la mia passione<br>con la sua dolce aria. Di là l'ho seguita—<br>o piuttosto essa m'ha attratto. Ma è svanita.<br>No, ricomincia. |
| *(didascalia)* | Canzone |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | A cinque tese di fondo giace tuo padre. Delle sue ossa è fatto corallo;<br>sono perle quelli che erano i suoi occhi;<br>nulla di lui che si dissolva<br>che non subisca una marina metamorfosi<br>in qualcosa di ricco e strano.<br>Le ninfe del mare suonano d'ora in ora la sua campana a morto: |
| *(didascalia)* | SPIRITI (da dentro) Din don. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Udite, ora li sento. |
| *(didascalia)* | SPIRITI (da dentro) |
| *(didascalia)* | Din-don campana. ecc. |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | La cantilena ricorda il mio padre annegato.<br>Questa non è faccenda mortale, né suono<br>che appartenga alla terra. |
| *(didascalia)* | Musica |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | Ora la sento sopra di me. |
| PROSPERO | (a [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]])<br>Solleva le frangiate cortine dei tuoi occhi,<br>e di' che cosa vedi laggiù. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Che cos'è? Uno spirito?<br>Signore, come si guarda intorno! Credetemi, signore,<br>ha una bella forma. Ma è uno spirito. |
| PROSPERO | No, ragazza, mangia e dorme, e ha sensi<br>quali abbiamo noi, proprio tali. Questo galante che vedi<br>era nel naufragio, e se non fosse un po' guastato<br>dal dolore, che è il tarlo della [[concepts/beauty\|bellezza]], potresti dire di<br>lui<br>che è una bella persona. Ha perduto i suoi compagni,<br>e va errando per ritrovarli. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Potrei chiamarlo<br>cosa divina, ché nulla di naturale<br>vidi mai così nobile. |
| PROSPERO | (a parte)<br>Procede, lo vedo,<br>come la mia [[motifs/soul\|anima]] lo suggerisce. (A [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]]) Spirito, fine spirito, ti<br>libererò |
| *(didascalia)* | entro due giorni, per questo. |
| *(didascalia)* | [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] a parte |
| PROSPERO | Certissimamente la dea<br>cui queste arie fanno corteo. (A [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]]) Concedete<br>alla mia [[motifs/prayer\|preghiera]] 425<br>di sapere se dimorate in quest'isola,<br>e che vogliate darmi qualche buon insegnamento<br>su come io debba comportarmi qui. La mia prima richiesta,<br>che pronuncio per ultima, è—o voi [[concepts/wonder\|meraviglia]]—<br>se siate fanciulla o no. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Nessuna [[concepts/wonder\|meraviglia]], signore,<br>ma certamente una fanciulla. |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | La mia lingua! Cieli!<br>Sono il primo fra quanti parlano questo idioma,<br>fossi soltanto dove lo si parla. |
| PROSPERO | Come, il primo?<br>Che saresti se il [[archetypes/king\|Re]] di Napoli t'udisse? |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | Una cosa sola, come sono adesso, che si stupisce<br>d'udirti parlare di Napoli. Egli mi ode,<br>e che m'oda io piango. Io stesso sono Napoli,<br>io che con questi occhi, mai da allora in riflusso, vidi<br>il [[archetypes/king\|Re]] mio padre naufragare. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Ahimè, [[concepts/mercy\|pietà]]! |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | Sì, in [[concepts/faith\|fede]], e tutti i suoi signori, il [[characters/duke-of-milan\|Duca di Milano]]<br>e il suo bravo figlio essendo due di loro. |
| PROSPERO | (a parte)<br>Il [[characters/duke-of-milan\|Duca di Milano]]<br>e la sua ancor più brava figlia potrebbero smentirti,<br>se ora fosse opportuno farlo. Al primo sguardo<br>si sono scambiati gli occhi.—Delicato [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]],<br>per questo ti farò libero. (A [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]]) Una parola, buon<br>signore. 445<br>[[concepts/fear\|Temo]] che vi siate fatto qualche torto. Una parola. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | (a parte)<br>Perché mio padre parla così sgarbatamente? Questo<br>è il terzo uomo che io abbia mai visto, il primo<br>per cui abbia mai sospirato. La pietà muova mio padre<br>a inclinare dalla mia parte. |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | Oh, se siete vergine,<br>e il vostro affetto non è già altrove, farò di voi<br>la [[characters/queen\|Regina]] di Napoli. |
| PROSPERO | Piano, signore! Un'altra parola.<br>(A parte) Sono l'uno in potere dell'altra. Ma questa rapida<br>faccenda<br>devo renderla malagevole, ché una conquista troppo lieve<br>non renda lieve il premio. (A [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]]) Un'altra parola. Ti<br>ordino<br>di darmi ascolto. Tu qui usurpi<br>un nome che non ti appartiene; e ti sei introdotto<br>in quest'isola come spia, per strapparla<br>a me che ne sono il signore. |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | No, com'è vero che sono un uomo. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Nulla di male può abitare in un tal tempio.<br>Se lo spirito maligno ha una casa così bella,<br>le cose buone si contenderanno d'abitarvi. |
| PROSPERO | (a [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]])<br>Seguimi.<br>(A [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]]) Non parlare tu per lui; è un traditore.<br>(A [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]]) Vieni!<br>T'ammanetterò il collo e i piedi insieme.<br>[[motifs/water\|Acqua]] di mare berrai; il tuo cibo saranno<br>le cozze del ruscello, radici avvizzite e i gusci<br>in cui la ghianda ebbe culla. Segui! |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | No.<br>Resisterò a un tale trattamento finché<br>il mio nemico non abbia più forza.<br>Sguaina la spada, ed è incantato e non può muoversi |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Oh caro padre,<br>non fate di lui prova troppo avventata, perché<br>è gentile, e non è da temere. |
| PROSPERO | Come, dico,<br>il mio piede mio maestro? Rinfodera la spada, traditore,<br>tu che fai mostra ma non osi colpire, tanto la tua<br>[[concepts/conscience\|coscienza]]<br>è posseduta dalla [[concepts/guilt\|colpa]]. Lascia la tua guardia,<br>ché posso qui disarmarti con questo bastone<br>e farti cadere l'arma. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Vi supplico, padre! |
| PROSPERO | Via! Non t'aggrappare alle mie vesti. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | Signore, abbiate pietà.<br>Sarò io la sua garante. |
| PROSPERO | Silenzio! Un'altra parola<br>mi farà rimproverarti, se non odiarti. Come,<br>avvocata d'un impostore? Zitta!<br>Credi che non vi siano altre forme come la sua,<br>avendo visto solo lui e [[characters/caliban\|Calibano]]. Sciocca ragazza!<br>Rispetto ai più degli uomini costui è un [[characters/caliban\|Calibano]],<br>e quelli, in confronto a lui, sono angeli. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | I miei affetti<br>sono dunque assai umili. Non ho [[concepts/ambition\|ambizione]]<br>di vedere un uomo più bello. |
| PROSPERO | (a [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]]) Vieni; obbedisci.<br>I tuoi nervi sono di nuovo nella loro infanzia,<br>e non hanno vigore in sé. |
| [[characters/ferdinand\|FERDINAND]] | Così sono.<br>I miei spiriti, come in un sogno, sono tutti legati.<br>La perdita di mio padre, la debolezza che sento,<br>il naufragio di tutti i miei amici, né le minacce di quest'uomo<br>al quale sono soggiogato, mi sarebbero che lievi,<br>potessi io solo, dalla mia [[motifs/prison\|prigione]], una volta al giorno<br>contemplare questa fanciulla. Di tutti gli altri angoli della terra<br>si serva la libertà; spazio abbastanza<br>ho in una tale [[motifs/prison\|prigione]]. |
| PROSPERO | (a parte)<br>Funziona. (A [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]]) Vieni.—<br>Hai fatto [[motifs/well\|bene]], fine [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]]. (A [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]]) Vieni<br>dietro a me.<br>(A [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]]) Odi che altro dovrai fare per me. |
| [[characters/miranda\|MIRANDA]] | (a [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]])<br>Fatevi coraggio.<br>Mio padre è di [[concepts/nature\|natura]] migliore, signore,<br>di quanto appaia dal suo parlare. È insolito<br>ciò che ora gli è uscito. |
| PROSPERO | (a [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]])<br>Sarai libero come<br>i venti dei monti; ma allora esegui esattamente<br>tutti i punti del mio comando. |
| [[characters/ariel\|ARIEL]] | Alla sillaba. |
| PROSPERO | (a [[characters/ferdinand\|Ferdinand]])<br>Vieni, seguimi. (A [[characters/miranda\|Miranda]]) Non parlare per lui. |
| *(didascalia)* | Escono |



<span class="atom-split" data-atom="act_2--scene_1" data-title="The Tempest — Act II, Scene 1" data-chapter="Scene 1" data-kind="scene" data-tags="topos/golden_age,archetype/fool,archetype/innocent,archetype/king,archetype/monster,archetype/tyrant,motif/clock,motif/crown,motif/death,motif/desert,motif/fire,motif/home,motif/love,motif/marriage,motif/memory,motif/moon,motif/nature,motif/sleep,motif/soul,motif/storm,motif/time,motif/water,motif/well,motif/wind,concept/ambition,concept/conscience,concept/doubt,concept/fear,concept/freedom,concept/honour,concept/hope,concept/imagination,concept/madness,concept/mercy,concept/nature,concept/poverty,concept/truth,concept/wonder,setting/desert,setting/italy,character/alonso,character/antonio,character/ariel,character/bottom,character/caliban,character/ferdinand,character/gonzalo,character/queen,character/sebastian,character/trinculo"></span>


| Speaker | Line |
|---|---|
| *(didascalia)* | Enter [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]], [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]], [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]], [[characters/gonzalo\|Gonzalo]], Adrian, and Francisco |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]])<br>Beseech you, sir, be merry. You have cause,<br>So have we all, of joy; for our escape<br>Is much beyond our loss. Our hint of woe<br>Is common; every day some sailor’s wife,<br>The masters of some merchant, and the merchant,<br>Have just our theme of woe. But for the miracle,<br>I mean our preservation, few in millions<br>Can speak like us. Then wisely, good sir, weigh<br>Our sorrow with our comfort. |
| [[characters/alonso\|ALONSO]] | Prithee, peace. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | (to [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]]) He receives comfort like cold porridge. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | The visitor will not give him o’er so. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | Look, he’s winding up the watch of his wit. By and by it will strike. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]]) Sir— |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | (to [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]]) One: tell. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]])<br>When every grief is entertained that’s offered,<br>Comes to th’entertainer— |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | A dollar. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | Dolour comes to him indeed. You have spoken truer than you purposed. |
| SEABASTIAN | You have taken it wiselier than I meant you should. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]]) Therefore my lord— |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | (to [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]]) Fie, what a spendthrift is he of his tongue! |
| [[characters/alonso\|ALONSO]] | (to [[characters/gonzalo\|Gonzalo]]) I prithee, spare. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | [[motifs/well\|Well]], I have done. But yet— |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | (to [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]]) He will be talking. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | Which of he or Adrian, for a good wager, first begins to crow? |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | The old cock. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | The cockerel. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | Done. The wager? |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | A laughter. 35 |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | A match! |
| ADRIAN | (to [[characters/gonzalo\|Gonzalo]]) Though this island seem to be [[motifs/desert\|desert]]— |
| *(didascalia)* | [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] (to [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]]) Ha, ha, ha! |
| *(didascalia)* | SEBASTIANSo, you’re paid. |
| ADRIAN | Uninhabitable, and almost inaccessible— |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | (to [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]]) Yet— |
| ADRIAN | Yet— |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | (to [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]]) He could not miss’t. |
| ADRIAN | It must needs be of subtle, tender, and delicate temperance. 45 |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | (to [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]]) Temperance was a delicate wench. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | Ay, and a subtle, as he most learnedly delivered. |
| ADRIAN | (to [[characters/gonzalo\|Gonzalo]]) The air breathes upon us here most sweetly. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | (to [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]]) As if it had lungs, and rotten ones. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | Or as ’twere perfumed by a fen. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to Adrian) Here is everything advantageous to life. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | (to [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]]) True, save means to live. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | Of that there’s none, or little. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to Adrian) How lush and lusty the grass looks! How green! |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | The ground indeed is tawny. |
| SEABASTIAN | With an eye of green in’t. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | He misses not much. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | No, he doth but mistake the [[concepts/truth\|truth]] totally. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to Adrian) But the rarity of it is, which is indeed almost beyond credit— |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | (to [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]]) As many vouched rarities are. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to Adrian) That our garments being, as they were, drenched in the sea, hold notwithstanding their freshness and glosses, being rather new-dyed than stained with salt [[motifs/water\|water]]. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | (to [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]]) If but one of his pockets could speak, would it not say he lies? |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | Ay, or very falsely pocket up his report. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to Adrian) Methinks our garments are now as fresh as when we put them on first in Afric, at the [[motifs/marriage\|marriage]] of the [[archetypes/king\|King]]’s fair daughter Claribel to the [[archetypes/king\|King]] of Tunis. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | ’Twas a sweet [[motifs/marriage\|marriage]], and we prosper [[motifs/well\|well]] in our return. |
| ADRIAN | Tunis was never graced before with such a paragon to their [[characters/queen\|queen]]. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | Not since widow Dido’s [[motifs/time\|time]]. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | (to [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]]) Widow? A pox o‘that! How came that ‘widow’ in? Widow Dido! |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | What if he had said ‘widower Aeneas’ too? Good Lord, how you take it! |
| ADRIAN | (to [[characters/gonzalo\|Gonzalo]]) ‘Widow Dido’ said you? You make me study of that: she was of Carthage, not of Tunis. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | This Tunis, sir, was Carthage. |
| ADRIAN | Carthage? |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | I assure you, Carthage. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | (to [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]]) His word is more than the miraculous harp. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | He hath raised the wall, and houses too. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | What impossible matter will he make easy next? |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | I think he will carry this island [[motifs/home\|home]] in his pocket, and give it his son for an apple. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | And sowing the kernels of it in the sea, bring forth more islands. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to Adrian) Ay. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | (to [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]]) Why, in good [[motifs/time\|time]]. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]]) Sir, we were talking that our garments seem now as fresh as when we were at Tunis, at the [[motifs/marriage\|marriage]] of your daughter, who is now [[characters/queen\|queen]]. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | And the rarest that e’er came there. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | Bate, I beseech you, widow Dido. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | O, widow Dido? Ay, widow Dido. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]]) Is not, sir, my doublet as fresh as the first day I wore it? I mean in a sort. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | (to [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]]) That ‘sort’ was [[motifs/well\|well]] fished for. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]]) When I wore it at your daughter’s [[motifs/marriage\|marriage]]. |
| [[characters/alonso\|ALONSO]] | You cram these words into mine ears against<br>The stomach of my sense. Would I had never<br>Married my daughter there! For, coming thence,<br>My son is lost; and, in my rate, she too,<br>Who is so far from [[settings/italy\|Italy]] removed<br>I ne’er again shall see her. O thou mine heir<br>Of Naples and of Milan, what strange fish<br>Hath made his meal on thee? |
| FRANCISCO | Sir, he may live.<br>I saw him beat the surges under him<br>And ride upon their backs. He trod the [[motifs/water\|water]],<br>Whose enmity he flung aside, and breasted<br>The surge, most swoll‘n, that met him. His bold head<br>’Bove the contentious waves he kept, and oared<br>Himself with his good arms in lusty stroke<br>To th’ shore, that o’er his wave-worn basis bowed,<br>As stooping to relieve him. I not [[concepts/doubt\|doubt]]<br>He came alive to land. |
| [[characters/alonso\|ALONSO]] | No, no; he’s gone. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | (to [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]])<br>Sir, you may thank yourself for this great loss,<br>That would not bless our Europe with your daughter,<br>But rather loose her to an African,<br>Where she, at least, is banished from your eye,<br>Who hath cause to wet the grief on’t. |
| [[characters/alonso\|ALONSO]] | Prithee, peace. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | You were kneeled to and importuned otherwise<br>By all of us, and the fair [[motifs/soul\|soul]] herself<br>Weighed between loathness and obedience at<br>Which end o’th’ beam should bow. We have lost your<br>son,<br>I [[concepts/fear\|fear]], for ever. Milan and Naples have<br>More widows in them of this business’ making<br>Than we bring men to comfort them. The fault’s your<br>own. |
| [[characters/alonso\|ALONSO]] | So is the dear‘st o’th’ loss. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | My lord [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]],<br>The [[concepts/truth\|truth]] you speak doth lack some gentleness<br>And [[motifs/time\|time]] to speak it in. You rub the sore<br>When you should bring the plaster. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | (to [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]]) Very [[motifs/well\|well]]. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | And most chirurgeonly. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]])<br>It is foul weather in us all, good sir,<br>When you are cloudy. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | (to [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]]) Fowl weather? |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | Very foul. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]])<br>Had I plantation of this isle, my lord— |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | (to Seabastian)<br>He’d sow’t with nettle-seed. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | Or docks, or mallows. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | And were the [[archetypes/king\|king]] on’t, what would I do? |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | (to [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]])<br>Scape being drunk, for want of wine. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | I’th’ commonwealth I would by contraries<br>Execute all things. For no kind of traffic<br>Would I admit, no name of magistrate;<br>Letters should not be known; riches, [[concepts/poverty\|poverty]],<br>And use of service, none; contract, succession,<br>Bourn, bound of land, tilth, vineyard, none;<br>No use of metal, corn, or wine, or oil;<br>No occupation, all men idle, all;<br>And women too—but [[archetypes/innocent\|innocent]] and pure;<br>No sovereignty— |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | (to [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]]) Yet he would be [[archetypes/king\|king]] on’t. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | The latter end of his commonwealth forgets the beginning. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]])<br>All things in common [[concepts/nature\|nature]] should produce<br>Without sweat or endeavour. Treason, felony,<br>[[works/sword-(chesterton)\|Sword]], pike, knife, gun, or need of any engine,<br>Would I not have; but [[concepts/nature\|nature]] should bring forth<br>Of it own kind all foison, all abundance,<br>To feed my [[archetypes/innocent\|innocent]] people. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | (to [[characters/antonio\|Antonio]]) No marrying ’mong his subjects? |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | None, man, all idle: whores and knaves. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]])<br>I would with such perfection govern, sir,<br>T’excel the [[topoi/golden-age\|Golden Age]]. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | Save his majesty! |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | Long live Gonzalol |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | (to [[characters/alonso\|Alonso]]) And—do you mark me, sir? |
| [[characters/alonso\|ALONSO]] | Prithee, no more. Thou dost talk nothing to me. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | I do [[motifs/well\|well]] believe your highness, and did it to minister occasion to these gentlemen, who are of such sensible and nimble lungs that they always use to laugh at nothing. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | ’Twas you we laughed at. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | Who, in this kind of merry fooling, am nothing to you. So you may continue, and laugh at nothing still. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | What a blow was there given! |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | An it had not fallen flat-long. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | You are gentlemen of brave mettle. You would lift the [[motifs/moon\|moon]] out of her sphere, if she would continue in it five weeks without changing. |
| *(didascalia)* | Enter [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]], invisible, playing solemn music |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | We would so, and then go a-bat-fowling. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | (to [[characters/gonzalo\|Gonzalo]]) Nay, good my lord, be not angry. |
| [[characters/gonzalo\|GONZALO]] | No, I warrant you, I will not adventure my discretion so weakly. Will you laugh me asleep? For I am very heavy. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | Go [[motifs/sleep\|sleep]], and hear us.<br>[[characters/gonzalo\|Gonzalo]],<br>Adrian, and Francisco [[motifs/sleep\|sleep]] |
| [[characters/alonso\|ALONSO]] | What, all so soon asleep? I wish mine eyes<br>Would, with themselves, shut up my thoughts.—I find<br>They are inclined to do so. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | Please you, sir,<br>Do not omit the heavy offer of it.<br>It seldom visits sorrow; when it doth,<br>It is a comforter. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | We two, my lord,<br>Will guard your person while you take your rest,<br>And watch your safety. |
| [[characters/alonso\|ALONSO]] | Thank you. Wondrous heavy. |
| *(didascalia)* | He sleeps. Exit [[characters/ariel\|Ariel]] |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | What a strange drowsiness possesses them! |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | It is the quality o’th’ climate. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | Why<br>Doth it not then our eyelids sink? I find<br>Not myself disposed to [[motifs/sleep\|sleep]]. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | Nor I; my spirits are nimble.<br>They fell together all, as by consent;<br>They dropped as by a thunderstroke. What might,<br>Worthy [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]], O, what might—? No more!—<br>And yet methinks I see it in thy face.<br>What thou shouldst be th’occasion speaks thee, and<br>My strong [[concepts/imagination\|imagination]] sees a [[motifs/crown\|crown]]<br>Dropping upon thy head. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | What, art thou waking? |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | Do you not hear me speak? |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | I do, and surely<br>It is a sleepy language, and thou speak’st<br>Out of thy [[motifs/sleep\|sleep]]. What is it thou didst say?<br>This is a strange repose, to be asleep<br>With eyes wide open; standing, speaking, moving,<br>And yet so fast asleep. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | Noble [[characters/sebastian\|Sebastian]],<br>Thou letst thy fortune [[motifs/sleep\|sleep]], die rather; wink’st<br>Whiles thou art waking. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | Thou dost snore distinctly;<br>There’s meaning in thy snores. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | I am more serious than my custom. You<br>Must be so too if heed me, which to do<br>Trebles thee o’er. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | [[motifs/well\|Well]], I am standing [[motifs/water\|water]]. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | I’ll teach you how to flow. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | Do so; to ebb<br>Hereditary sloth instructs me. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | O,<br>If you but knew how you the purpose cherish<br>Whiles thus you mock it; how in stripping it<br>You more invest it! Ebbing men, indeed,<br>Most often do so near the [[characters/bottom\|bottom]] run<br>By their own [[concepts/fear\|fear]] or sloth. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | Prithee, say on.<br>The setting of thine eye and cheek proclaim<br>A matter from thee, and a birth, indeed,<br>Which throes thee much to yield. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | Thus, sir.<br>Although this lord of weak remembrance, this,<br>Who shall be of as little [[motifs/memory\|memory]]<br>When he is earthed, hath here almost persuaded—<br>For he’s a spirit of persuasion, only<br>Professes to persuade—the [[archetypes/king\|King]] his son’s alive,<br>’Tis as impossible that he’s undrowned<br>As he that sleeps here swims. |
| [[characters/sebastian\|SEBASTIAN]] | I have no [[concepts/hope\|hope]]<br>That he’s undrowned. |
| [[characters/antonio\|ANTONIO]] | O, out of that ‘no [[concepts/hope\|hope]]’<br>What great [[concepts/hope\|hope]] have you! No [[concepts/hope\|hope]] that way is<br>Another way so high a [[concepts/hope\|hope]] that even<br>[[concepts/ambition\|Ambition]] cannot pierce a wink beyond,<br>But [[concepts/doubt\|doubt]] discovery there.
